rating: 4
review_text: Super cute, beachy place for some yummy drinks (alcoholic and non). There’s picnic tables on the porch and a couple swings with a kid area, more picnic tables down below the deck covered with umbrellas, and also plenty seating inside. Large variety of drinks, i tried one of the lemonade teas which was delicious and also one of the popping boba teas. The sweet potato fries were delicious. The dunked wedge with chicken tasted as expected, and the chicken was juicy. Unfortunately, the Mediterranean bowl didn’t met expectations. Overall, it was a good experience but there’s room for improvement in flavor combos.

reviewer_name: Andrea Van Someren
rating: 5
body: Arrived after a day at the beach and the place was pretty packed. Mostly people playing bingo, others enjoying their food. The girl at the counter was very welcoming and friendly. We ordered waters and when they gave us bottles, she said it was on them. For some reason that blew my mind in a little tourist town. I was grateful and gladly accepted it. My BLT with chips and salsa were pretty good. I would say the BLT was up in the top 3 all time BLTs for me. 👌🏼 They didn’t skimp on the bacon. *The Beach bowl was delicious according to my partner. I wanted to enjoy all of my BLT, so I didn’t try it, but it looked goooood!

entity_slug: picnic-beach-bar-grill
reviewer_name: Jessi Butler
rating: 5
body: My family and I came here for the first time for lunch before flying out and we were pleasantly surprised by how great the food was! I myself got The Beach Bowl, which came with a large piece of grilled salmon, jasmine rice, and roasted cauliflower, zucchini, and squash all with an Asian Glaze on top. It was absolutely delicious! My sister got the Tuna Poke Bowl and I was able to snag a bite of it, and it was so fresh and delicious as well! The atmosphere is very chill and relaxing, with both indoor & outdoor seating options, which is great for anytime of the year. Music was fire too! We will definitely be back next time we are in the area!

--- entity_reviews [row 4 of 5] ---
id: 80a674a1-05de-4973-a18b-ff8459ddb979
entity_slug: picnic-beach-bar-grill
reviewer_name: Ashley Morikawa
rating: 4
body: Super cute, beachy place for some yummy drinks (alcoholic and non). There’s picnic tables on the porch and a couple swings with a kid area, more picnic tables down below the deck covered with umbrellas, and also plenty seating inside. Large variety of drinks, i tried one of the lemonade teas which was delicious and also one of the popping boba teas. The sweet potato fries were delicious. The dunked wedge with chicken tasted as expected, and the chicken was juicy. Unfortunately, the Mediterranean bowl didn’t met expectations. There was a lot in the bowl but the flavors didn’t mesh well together and the fish was dry. The servers were very friendly. Overall, it was a good experience but there’s room for improvement in flavor combos.

--- entity_reviews [row 5 of 5] ---
id: 3059d749-0d14-4c2f-8a3d-6516c61b80a2
entity_slug: picnic-beach-bar-grill
reviewer_name: Bryon Harris
rating: 4
body: We visited this past August. This was our first time in the area and we wanted to try something local with a view.  The food was good.  I had the calamari and a grouper sandwich.  Mom had the fried Gulf shrimp basket.  We thought all three tasted great and were fresh.  I hope you like steps, because there's plenty of them to go inside.  Once inside, they have no customer service.  You have to order at the counter and pick up your food when your order is done.  For the prices they charge, you expect some kind of service, but forget about it, serve yourself.  I just found this to be ridiculous.  At least I didn't feel the need to tip anyone but myself, and I'm a good tipper.  The view was okay from that height, but all you can really see are other businesses.
